- **tax-task-track-state-residency**: ADVANCED / OPTIONAL — for multistate workers, snowbirds, recent movers, and remote workers in border states. Logs days-per-state to substantiate residency or non-residency at filing. Most states use a 183-day threshold (sometimes 184) plus a domicile test. Some no-income-tax states (FL, TX, WA, NV, TN, SD, WY, AK, NH) attract aggressive residency challenges from prior high-tax states (e.g., NY, CA). Flags as user approaches state-specific thresholds. ([fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life](https://github.com/fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life/tree/HEAD/paperclip-pkg/skills/tax-task-track-state-residency) · ★17 · 2 copies)

- **taxonomic-coverage-extrapolation**: Use when the goal is to measure reference database coverage gaps for a taxonomic group (e.g. Insecta genera with full-length CO1 species-rank sequences) and project when the database will saturate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-coverage-extrapolation))
- **taxonomic-database-construction**: Use when raw shotgun reads from complex biological samples (e.g., food) must be classified against a large multi-kingdom reference genome collection, and the k-mer/minhash database first has to be constructed at scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-database-construction))
- **taxonomic-description-sentence-segmentation**: Use when input is natural-language prokaryotic taxonomic description text (e.g., the Part_One_111_final dataset) that must be segmented into sentences and normalized before phenotypic character extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-description-sentence-segmentation))
- **taxonomic-description-text-mining**: Use when you have a folder of unstructured prokaryote taxonomic description documents and need to populate a taxon-by-character matrix of phenotypic characters (e.g., cell dimensions, Gram stain, NaCl/pH/temperature optima) for downstream comparative analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-description-text-mining))
- **taxonomic-distance-computation**: Use when you need to explain per-habitat species-level classification error rates (e.g., from bespoke q2-clawback weights) by measuring how often similar sequences from the same habitat originate from different species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-distance-computation))
- **taxonomic-distance-reranking-of-candidates**: Use when annotating LC-MS/MS features of a biological extract against an in silico structure database and you want to reweight spectral matches by how closely the candidate's source organism relates taxonomically to the sample's biosource. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-distance-reranking-of-candidates))
- **taxonomic-distance-scoring**: Use when you have ranked candidate annotation lists (e.g. from ISDB-DNP, Sirius or MS-Finder) plus resolved taxonomy of the producing organism, and want to re-rank candidates using taxonomic distance to improve precision, recall and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-distance-scoring))
- **taxonomic-distribution-aggregation**: Use when you have a filtered DiSCo hitlist (disco_hitlist.tsv) of DsrA/DsrB hits from (meta)genomic records and need to summarize which lineages and phyla carry the Dsr pathway. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-distribution-aggregation))
- **taxonomic-distribution-grouping**: Use when you have per-feature taxonomy assignments (e.g. QIIME2 Naive Bayes or BLAST predictions on 16S mock-community data) and need abundance profiles grouped at a chosen taxonomic level for composition comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-distribution-grouping))
- **taxonomic-feature-extraction-by-level**: Use when the input is a merged MetaPhlAn or QIIME OTU/BIOM table containing multiple taxonomic ranks and you need a feature matrix at a specific level (genus, species, or all) before filtering, normalization, or model building in MegaR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-feature-extraction-by-level))
- **taxonomic-group-aggregation**: Use when a FASTA-derived per-sequence statistics table needs to be summarized by taxonomic labels (phylum) or molecule type (chromosome vs plasmid), e.g. to reproduce reported dataset statistics before model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-group-aggregation))
- **taxonomic-hierarchy-sunburst-rendering**: Use when tab-separated Kraken2 kreport2 files are available and the goal is interactive exploration of read distribution across taxonomic ranks (Sankey/Sunburst/species table) for one or more barcoded samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-hierarchy-sunburst-rendering))
- **taxonomic-hierarchy-tree-traversal**: Use when GCN (or another trait) must be mapped onto the Greengenes phylogeny or taxonomic tree to test phylogenetic signal or infer trait values for taxa lacking sequenced relatives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-hierarchy-tree-traversal))
- **taxonomic-hit-aggregation**: Use when microbeMASST/jobs.py batch search output (_matches.tsv, _datasets.tsv, _count_microbe.tsv) must be parsed and condensed into a per-compound table of taxa and unique sample counts to answer which organisms match a molecule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-hit-aggregation))
- **taxonomic-identifier-mapping**: Use when building a k-mer-to-LCA taxonomy database from NCBI RefSeq genomes so each k-mer in a metagenomic classifier resolves to a specific NCBI taxon ID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-identifier-mapping))
- **taxonomic-interest-classification**: Use when classifying a user's interests from a text corpus, measuring interest diversity or concentration, mapping content to hierarchical categories, determining whether a profile is polymathic or specialist, or computing orthogonality scores across interest domains ([aaddrick/written-voice-replication](https://github.com/aaddrick/written-voice-replication/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/taxonomic-interest-classification) · ★14)
- **taxonomic-label-vocabulary-matching**: Use when a hierarchical 16S rRNA classifier (e.g. DeepTaxa on Greengenes2 2024.09) must be evaluated on test sequences whose species labels are absent from the training vocabulary, to measure parent-rank robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-label-vocabulary-matching))
- **taxonomic-level-assignment-of-chemical-categories**: Use when curating or rebuilding a chemical classification ontology from OBO-format term stanzas, and each term must be assigned to a taxonomic level with preferred names and synonyms resolved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-level-assignment-of-chemical-categories))
- **taxonomic-lineage-aggregation**: Use when microbeMASST batch-search outputs (_matches.tsv, _count_microbe.tsv) must be aggregated per NCBI lineage rank and rendered as an interactive D3 taxonomic tree (microbe_tree.html) showing which microbes may produce a queried metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-lineage-aggregation))
- **taxonomic-lineage-assignment**: Use when similarity matrices between reads and reference genomes have been computed (e.g. by LiME's ClusterLCP/ClusterBWT_DA) and each read must be assigned to a TaxID at a specific rank such as species or phylum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-lineage-assignment))
- **taxonomic-lineage-propagation**: Use when building counts matrices from profiler/reference pairings (QIIME 2, Mothur, PathoScope 2, Kraken 2 vs. SILVA 138, Greengenes 13_8, RefSeq) and feature taxonomy strings contain empty rank fields above the deepest assigned label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-lineage-propagation))
- **taxonomic-lineage-retrieval-and-formatting**: Use when building a CO1 (or similar marker gene) reference training set from NCBI-derived sequences and each retained sequence needs its associated taxonomic lineage formatted into RDP taxonomy files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-lineage-retrieval-and-formatting))
- **taxonomic-metadata-degradation-simulation**: Use when you need to validate or re-tune taxonomically informed re-ranking scores (e.g., taxo_scorer weights) under degraded or incomplete taxonomic metadata, testing robustness of the scoring strategy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-metadata-degradation-simulation))
- **taxonomic-metadata-integration**: Use when after organism name cleaning and standardization (via 1_cleaningOriginal.R and 4_cleaningTaxonomy.R) has produced a cleaned organism table (interim/tables/2_cleaned/organism/cleaned.tsv.gz), apply this skill to map those cleaned names to Open Tree of Life (OTL) identifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/taxonomic-metadata-integration) · 2 copies)
- **taxonomic-metadata-mapping**: Use when genome-to-taxon mapping is needed to rank catalogue elements (e.g. ISC insertion sequences) by how many genera and species their hits span across an indexed genome database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-metadata-mapping))
- **taxonomic-metadata-resolution**: Use when processing LC-MS/MS DDA metabolomics samples sample-wise and the input metadata only contains free-text organism binomial names that must be standardized to stable taxonomic identifiers before RDF/SPARQL integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-metadata-resolution))
- **taxonomic-misassignment-root-cause-analysis**: Use when a metagenomic/16S profiler reports high-confidence assignments to an unexpected taxon whose abundance correlates with a known community member (here Pearson r = 0.959), suggesting the reference library—not the reads—carries a contaminant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-misassignment-root-cause-analysis))
- **taxonomic-name-matching**: Use when you have a resolved species list from eDNA metabarcoding and need to verify plausibility by checking each taxon against independent occurrence records for the sampling region (e.g., locality or island-level faunistic data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-name-matching))
- **taxonomic-name-resolution-against-backbone**: Use when a structural database or annotation output contains free-text organism names and you need each name resolved to a species/genus/family position on a taxonomy backbone (Catalogue of Life) before taxonomically informed re-ranking of candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-name-resolution-against-backbone))
- **taxonomic-name-resolution-opentree**: Use when a metadata table's ATTRIBUTE_Species (or genus/family) names may be outdated or synonymized and those names will drive taxon-level database queries such as LOTUS compound counting for the Inventa LC score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-name-resolution-opentree))
- **taxonomic-nestedness-nodf-analysis**: Use when you have a BIOM abundance table plus sample metadata (e.g., EMP environment types non-saline/saline/animal/plant) and need to test whether taxa at phylum, class, or order level are gained but not lost with increasing sample richness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-nestedness-nodf-analysis))
- **taxonomic-nomenclature-normalization-and-validation**: Use when you have organism names originating from 31+ heterogeneous natural product databases that use different taxonomic authorities, nomenclature versions, or rank assignments, and you need to harmonize them into a single organism subgraph with validated ranks before linking to chemical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/taxonomic-nomenclature-normalization-and-validation))
- **taxonomic-phylum-distribution-tabulation**: Use when a metagenomic genome-reconstruction study reports draft/complete genome counts and per-phylum assignments (e.g. from 16S rRNA or SCG-binned genomes) that must be extracted, verified, and tabulated into a structured distribution table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-phylum-distribution-tabulation))
- **taxonomic-phylum-kingdom-counting**: Use when you have a directory of structured JSON database entries with taxonomy fields and need to compute diversity counts (distinct phyla/kingdoms) to validate reported database statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-phylum-kingdom-counting))
- **taxonomic-probe-count-tabulation**: Use when you have a published probe/bait FASTA plus genome-to-taxon metadata and need to compute what fraction of strains are covered at genus vs. species level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-probe-count-tabulation))
- **taxonomic-profile-benchmarking**: Use when you need to reproduce or compare strain- and species-level read-assignment accuracy (precision, recall, precision2) and composition-estimation accuracy (r2, L1 distance) of long-read metagenomic classifiers on mock or simulated communities with known ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profile-benchmarking))
- **taxonomic-profile-generation**: Use when metagenomic sequencing reads (fastq/fasta) must be converted into taxonomic profiles (relative abundances and per-read assignments) for benchmarking or abundance estimation of gut microbiome taxa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profile-generation))
- **taxonomic-profile-l1-error-computation**: Use when you need to quantitatively rank taxonomic profilers by L1 norm error against a known ground-truth profile for a specific CAMI sample, at a chosen taxonomic rank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-profile-l1-error-computation))
- **taxonomic-profile-parsing**: Use when input is a CAMI-format taxonomic profile (taxID, rank, abundance per taxon) that must be mapped onto the NCBI taxdump tree, e.g. for TAMPA-style tree annotation or profiler comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-profile-parsing))
- **taxonomic-profile-threshold-filtering**: Use when a merged MetaPhlAn table (e.g. merged_metaphlan_t1d.txt) or QIIME OTU/BIOM table must be filtered to informative features before training GLM, SVM, or RF classifiers in MegaR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profile-threshold-filtering))
- **taxonomic-profiler-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when choosing among candidate taxonomic profilers or reference databases for metagenomic profiling, and benchmark results (e.g. CAMI profiler outputs, KMA .mapstat files) need to be scored and compared. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profiler-benchmark-evaluation))
- **taxonomic-profiler-benchmark-visualization**: Use when you have a per-pairing benchmark metrics table (sensitivity, specificity, error score, alpha diversity deviation for each profiler-library combination) and need to determine which metrics are correlated and which pairings occupy the high-sensitivity/high-specificity quadrant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profiler-benchmark-visualization))
- **taxonomic-profiling-with-marker-genes**: Use when metagenomic sequencing reads (post quality-control) need organism-level phylogenetic characterization prior to or alongside gene-catalog mapping, as in the CAMAMED case-control colorectal cancer gut metagenome workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-profiling-with-marker-genes))
- **taxonomic-rank-annotation**: Use when a QC'd eDNA OTU table contains taxonomic assignments at mixed ranks and you need Family-or-higher agglomeration, per-row rank annotation, and marine/terrestrial filtering before reporting taxon counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-rank-annotation))
- **taxonomic-rank-constrained-tree-plotting**: Use when you have one or more taxonomic profiler profiles plus a ground-truth CAMI-style profile and need to visualize which clades at a chosen rank (e.g., class, phylum) drive profiler errors or abundance differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-rank-constrained-tree-plotting))
- **taxonomic-rank-precision-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a taxonomic classifier's precision and sensitivity at species/genus/family/phylum ranks on simulated reads from organisms whose clades were excluded from the reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-rank-precision-evaluation))
- **taxonomic-rank-pruning-and-count-propagation**: Use when you have per-read taxonomic assignments from a MetaCacheSpark/AFS-MetaCache query run and need read counts and abundance percentages per organism at a chosen rank via the -a/--abundance_per parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-rank-pruning-and-count-propagation))
- **taxonomic-rank-tally-validation**: Use when reproducing or validating taxonomic classification counts of vOTUs from virome or shotgun metagenome assemblies, where expected per-rank tallies (phylum through genus/species) must be matched against published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-rank-tally-validation))
- **taxonomic-read-count-aggregation**: Use when Kraken2 per-sample report files (.kreport2.txt) must be parsed to extract exact read counts for watchlist taxids (e.g., E. coli 562, S. aureus 1280, B. anthracis 1392, Y. pestis 632, C. botulinum 1491) feeding downstream validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-read-count-aggregation))
- **taxonomic-read-flow-sankey-visualization**: Use when per-sample Kraken2 kreport2 files are available and the goal is to explore a sample's taxonomic composition interactively, e.g. inspecting how sequencing reads distribute across rank levels during an Oxford Nanopore run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-read-flow-sankey-visualization))
- **taxonomic-read-mapping-validation**: Use when a newly built taxonomic database (e.g. fungal marker genes) must be shown to reject reads from off-target organisms such as bacteria before deployment on real metagenomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-read-mapping-validation))
- **taxonomic-redundancy-quantification**: Use when validating OTU tables (raw or LULU-curated, e.g. from VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP or DADA2) against ground-truth survey data, and you need to measure how many OTUs redundantly represent the same species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-redundancy-quantification))
- **taxonomic-sequence-classification**: Use when assigning genus-rank taxonomy to primer-anchored CO1 amplicon sequences (~100-650 bp) and you need high-confidence assignments with a controlled false positive rate rather than accepting the top BLAST hit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-sequence-classification))
- **taxonomic-shift-detection**: Use when analyzing longitudinal content data for interest migration patterns, detecting when a user or entity shifts from one category subgroup to another over time, identifying change points in categorical distributions, determining which historical era best represents the current voice, or measuring the magnitude and permanence of topic drift across temporal windows ([aaddrick/written-voice-replication](https://github.com/aaddrick/written-voice-replication/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/taxonomic-shift-detection) · ★14)
- **taxonomic-specificity-classification**: Use when you have a deposited probe set (e.g. from the HUBDesign probes/Coronavirus directory) plus the annotated source genomes, and need to determine what fraction of probes are genome-specific versus cross-reactive across multiple viruses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-specificity-classification))
- **taxonomic-tie-resolution-via-lca**: Use when implementing or auditing k-mer-based taxonomic label assignment where root-to-leaf path scores can tie, and a deterministic, conservative label (the LCA of tied leaves) must be emitted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/taxonomic-tie-resolution-via-lca))
- **taxonomic-training-set-parsing**: Use when you hold two or more RDP classifier training sets (a taxonomy file plus a FASTA file each) and need to verify claims about how many taxa and reference sequences each covers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/taxonomic-training-set-parsing))
- **taxonomic-tree-generation-for-graphlan**: Use when you have a taxonomy-structured abundance or biomarker table (MetaPhlAn/LEfSe/HUMAnN output, BIOM v1/v2, e.g. 16S vaginal microbiota) and need a GraPhlAn tree + annotation pair for circular rendering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-tree-generation-for-graphlan))
- **taxonomic-tree-plotting-ete3**: Use when standardized taxonomic profiles (profiler output plus a gold-standard profile with TaxIDs) need a rank-resolved tree visualization of per-clade relative abundances, e.g., MetaPhlAn vs mOTUs on the CAMI marmgCAMI2 marine sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomic-tree-plotting-ete3))
- **taxonomic-weighting-in-annotation**: Use when you have a feature table with candidate metabolite annotations (m/z, retention time, chemical identifiers) and MS/MS spectra, and you know the organism or taxon of origin for your samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/taxonomic-weighting-in-annotation))
- **taxonomically-informed-annotation-reranking**: Use when MS/MS annotations from ISDB-DNP, Sirius or MS-Finder exist for spectra of a taxonomically known biological source and correct structures sit at low spectral-matching ranks, so re-ranking with taxonomic distance can boost rank-1 recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/taxonomically-informed-annotation-reranking))
- **taxonomist**: Analyze and optimize a WordPress site's category taxonomy. Exports all posts, uses AI to suggest an improved category structure — merging duplicates, retiring dead categories, creating missing ones, writing descriptions, and re-categorizing posts. Packages `/aidlc --stage tcms-test-cases --single`: the engine emits one run-stage directive for tcms-test-cases and its gate, the conductor runs it, then the single-stage run commits a synthetic-id pair and stops.
